Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) has emerged as a pivotal technique for improving language models by incorporating external knowledge at inference time. As device-cloud collaborative inference makes it feasible to deploy small language models on edge devices, a new setting arises in which private documents remain on the device and public knowledge resides in the cloud. Privacy and policy constraints often forbid raw document exchange, creating a document-isolated dual-end RAG setting. However, existing methods rely on frequent remote synchronization and dense evidence transfer, limiting throughput under realistic latency and bandwidth conditions. To address this issue, we propose CONCORD, an asynchronous sparse aggregation framework for dual-end RAG under document isolation. CONCORD treats the cloud as an asynchronously arriving evidence source rather than a continuously synchronized co-generator. Specifically, we introduce waiting debt control to decide whether each decoding step should continue waiting for remote participation based on the observed return of waiting. We also design a certificate-guided minimal supplementation mechanism that requests only the remote evidence needed to determine the current greedy decision. Steps that consult the cloud preserve the same greedy token as dense dual-end aggregation, while the remaining steps commit locally without remote evidence. Experiments on Natural Questions and WikiText-2 show that CONCORD improves end-to-end throughput over baselines by $1.66\times$ and $2.15\times$, respectively, while reducing per-token communication by over two orders of magnitude and maintaining comparable answer quality and perplexity.

</details>


### [93] [CogGuard: Cognitive and Operational Profiling for Proactive Warning in Edge Intelligent Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15199)
*Zhi Yao,Weihao Chen,Zhiqing Tang,Hanshuai Cui,Qianli Ma,Weijia Jia,Wei Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CogGuard是一个面向边缘智能服务的主动预警框架，将离线LLM构建用户画像与在线SLM预测解耦，通过前缀对齐的KV缓存复用和长度感知的分布式微调策略，在教育表现预测和操作任务结果预测两个场景中实现高效部署，分别将画像构建时间降低48%、分布式微调时间降低19%，在百分制预警任务中MAE分别达到13.4和5.9。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有边缘智能服务的主动预警方案面临两个核心挑战：(1) 用户画像方法通常是领域特化的，缺乏跨服务场景的可复用抽象；(2) 在异构边缘集群上微调对齐模型时，因输入序列长度差异大导致同步开销过高。

Method: CogGuard提出"离线LLM画像构建→在线SLM分数预测"的解耦架构，核心方法包括：(1) 设计场景特化的画像方法配合前缀对齐的KV缓存复用以减少重复编码开销；(2) 提出长度感知的分布式微调策略，结合对比正则化以缓解异构集群上的负载不均。

Result: 在教育数据集和操作数据集上，CogGuard将画像构建时间降低最多48%，分布式微调时间降低19%；在百分制预警任务中MAE分别达到13.4和5.9；在大规模教育场景中，相比最强基线预测误差降低15.4%。

Conclusion: CogGuard通过静态-动态画像到分数的共享流水线，成功实现了跨场景的预警能力，验证了LLM与SLM协同的边端部署范式在降低开销的同时保持预测性能的有效性。

Abstract: Proactive warning is an important capability for edge intelligent services, where the system predicts whether a subject will successfully complete an incoming task under strict latency and privacy constraints. Such prediction depends on both long-term static attributes and short-term dynamic states derived from historical interaction logs. Recent Large Language Models (LLMs) offer strong long-context reasoning for constructing structured profiles from these logs, but existing solutions face two challenges for edge deployment: (1) profiling methods are typically domain-specific and lack a reusable abstraction across service scenarios, and (2) fine-tuning alignment models on heterogeneous edge clusters incurs high synchronization overhead due to the variance in input sequence lengths. To address these challenges, we propose CogGuard, a proactive-warning framework for edge intelligent services. CogGuard decouples offline LLM-based profile construction from online Small Language Model (SLM)-based score prediction through a shared static-dynamic profile-to-score pipeline, and instantiates it in two representative scenarios: educational performance warning and operational task outcome warning. For efficient profile construction, we design scenario-specific profiling methods with prefix-aligned KV-cache reuse to reduce repeated encoding overhead. For edge-side model alignment, we propose a length-aware distributed fine-tuning strategy with contrastive regularization to mitigate workload imbalance on heterogeneous clusters. Experiments on education and operation datasets show that CogGuard reduces profile construction time by up to 48% and distributed fine-tuning time by 19%, while achieving MAEs of 13.4 and 5.9, respectively, on 100-point-scale warning tasks. In the largest educational setting, CogGuard reduces prediction error by 15.4% compared with the strongest baseline.

</details>


### [94] [Attribute Inference from Interactive Targeted Ads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15209)
*Peihao Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了交互式定向广告中用户行为暴露导致的属性推断隐私风险，构建了基于噪声oracle的可复现基准，并通过贝叶斯、监督学习、正无标签学习和自适应攻击等方法的评估，发现披露策略是最有效的隐私控制手段，在160次广告活动下攻击AUC约0.64-0.65。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式定向广告中，用户的可见行为（如点击、浏览）与特定广告活动关联后，广告主可能获得与用户个体绑定的观察数据，而不仅仅是聚合报告。这种机制构成了一个噪声oracle，可用于推断用户敏感属性。现有研究缺乏对这一隐私泄露通道的规范化建模和可复现的评估框架。

Method: 作者构建了包含目标谓词、曝光、交互和披露四个边界的噪声oracle模型，区分了资格与投放、交互与广告主可见性之间的差距。使用与公开数据校准的合成人群生成已知敏感标签的地面真值，并构建广告活动语义层提供主题变体和响应先验。评估了贝叶斯攻击、监督学习攻击、正无标签攻击和自适应攻击四种方法。

Result: 在4种主题变体、7个模拟种子和2种交互设置下，重复160次广告活动后，贝叶斯和监督攻击在主要设置中AUC约0.64，高交互设置中约0.65。披露策略是最强的控制手段，聚合报告完全消除oracle输入，类型过滤和随机化披露可减少泄露信号。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一个针对交互式定向广告隐私保护的可复现模型、测试工具和防御评估方法。核心结论是：用户身份暴露的重复广告活动会产生可测量但有界的推断信号，披露策略（聚合报告、类型过滤、随机化披露）是控制隐私泄露的关键手段。

Abstract: Targeted advertising systems can pair audiences selected by advertisers with ad units that expose visible user actions. When an interaction remains linked to the campaign that elicited it, the advertiser may receive an observation tied to a user rather than only an aggregate report. We model that channel as a noisy oracle for attribute inference. The model separates targeting predicates, exposure, interaction, and disclosure. These boundaries capture the gap between eligibility and delivery, and the gap between interaction and advertiser visibility.
  We build a reproducible benchmark using synthetic populations calibrated with public data, each with known sensitive labels. A generated campaign semantics layer provides topic variants and response priors. The simulator generates the ground truth, event traces, disclosed observations, and metrics. The evaluation compares Bayesian, supervised, positive and unlabeled, and adaptive attacks under common campaign and disclosure definitions.
  The final evaluation uses four topic variants, seven simulator seeds, and two interaction settings. Repeated campaigns with identity exposure produce measurable but bounded inference signal. At $160$ campaigns, Bayesian and supervised attacks reach about $0.64$ AUC in the main setting and about $0.65$ AUC in the higher interaction setting. Disclosure policy is the strongest control. Aggregate reporting removes the evaluated oracle input tied to users. Type filtering and randomized disclosure reduce the released signal. The result is a model, artifact, and defense evaluation method for privacy in interactive targeted advertising. The code is available at https://github.com/P-HOW/Interactive-Ad-Oracle.

</details>


### [95] [Visual-Seeker: Towards Visual-Native Multimodal Agentic Search via Active Visual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15231)
*Zhengbo Zhang,Changtao Miao,Jinbo Su,Zhaowen Zhou,Chunxia Zhang,Xukai Wang,Ruiqi Liu,Kaiyuan Zheng,Jiansheng Cai,Bo Zhang,Zhe Li,Shiming Xiang,Ying Yan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Visual-Seeker，一种视觉原生的多模态深度搜索智能体，通过主动视觉推理机制，在复杂开放场景中实现多跳跨模态搜索，在五个基准上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM在复杂开放场景中缺乏事实性基础，而现有的多模态深度搜索智能体主要依赖显式语义简单的图像和纯文本证据轨迹，限制了多跳跨模态推理与搜索能力，视觉原生的搜索范式尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出Visual-Seeker，将视觉作为主动输入而非静态输入，动态关注细粒度视觉细节并在搜索过程中持续收集视觉证据。设计主动视觉推理数据管道，合成5K高质量多模态轨迹用于模型训练。

Result: 在五个具有挑战性的多模态搜索基准上取得SOTA性能，超越多个专有模型，验证了其在真实网络环境中具有鲁棒的视觉原生推理与搜索能力。

Conclusion: 视觉原生的主动视觉推理范式能有效提升多模态深度搜索智能体在复杂开放场景中的事实性基础能力，代码与数据已开源。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have demonstrated impressive capabilities in many visual tasks, but they often struggle with factual grounding when confronted with complex, open-world scenarios. While recent multimodal deep search agents attempt to address this issue by utilizing external tools, the visual-native search paradigm remains underexplored. Existing methods primarily rely on simple images with explicit semantics and text-only evidence trajectories, limiting the agent's ability to perform multi-hop, cross-modal reasoning and search. To address these limitations, we propose Visual-Seeker, a visual-native multimodal deep search agent via active visual reasoning. Rather than treating vision as a static input, our agent actively attends to fine-grained visual details, dynamically harvests visual evidence throughout the search process. To unlock its visual-native potential, we design an active visual reasoning data pipeline and synthesize 5K high-quality multimodal trajectories for model training. Extensive experiments demonstrate the state-of-the-art performance across five challenging multimodal search benchmarks, even surpassing several proprietary models, validating robust visual-native reasoning and search in real-world web environments. The code and data can be accessed at: https://github.com/ZhengboZhang/Visual-Seeker.

</details>


### [96] [Mask-Proof: An LLM-based Automated Data Curation Pipeline on Mathematical Proofs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15258)
*Jierui Zhang,Siyuan Tan,Xinhang Li,Longzhuangzhi Lin,Dailin Li,Chengfeng Gu,Xinping Li,Yaxian Hao,Shengjia Liang,Yuxiang Ren,Wenhao Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Mask-Proof是一个将数学证明转化为可自动检查的掩码步骤任务的流水线，并构建了包含292个问题的Mask-ProofBench基准，用于可扩展、可复现地测量大语言模型在长证明中的逐步推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估方法要么只关注最终答案，要么依赖昂贵的人工评分，缺乏可扩展且可复现的方式来衡量大语言模型在多来源长证明中的逐步推理能力，这种评估差距限制了可信AI在证明验证的科学进步中的应用。

Method: 提出Mask-Proof流水线，通过掩码关键公式步骤并提供必要的上下文，将真实数学证明转化为可自动检查的掩码步骤任务；使用基于LLM的等价性判断器（含重复投票机制）评估模型重建结果。

Result: 构建了包含292个精选问题的Mask-ProofBench基准，覆盖多个研究领域；实验表明推理增强模型比标准模型性能高出12%至27%；评估器与人类专家标注者的一致率达到96.8%。

Conclusion: Mask-Proof能够实现忠实、可复现且可比较的逐步数学推理测量，为可信赖的AI辅助数学证明验证提供了有效评估工具。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly capable of mathematical problem solving and can even assist with research-level proofs, yet we still lack a scalable and reproducible way to measure step-level reasoning in long proofs across diverse sources. This evaluation gap limits trustworthy AI assistance in proof-certified scientific progress. Existing evaluations often emphasize final answers or rely on costly expert grading, while end-to-end proof generation remains open-ended and hard to verify automatically. We introduce Mask-Proof, a pipeline that turns real proofs into automatically checkable masked-step tasks. It masks key formula steps, provides the necessary surrounding context, and evaluates model reconstructions with an LLM-based equivalence judge using repeated votes for stability. The resulting Mask-ProofBench contains 292 curated problems across diverse research areas. Experiments with 17 models show that reasoning-enhanced models outperform standard models by 12% to 27%. Our evaluator achieves 96.8% agreement with expert annotators, enabling faithful, reproducible, and comparable measurement of step-level mathematical reasoning. Benchmark, annotations, and code are available at https://github.com/weating/Mask-Proof.

</details>


### [97] [Feature Attribution in Directed Acyclic Graphs Using Edge Intervention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15273)
*Qiheng Sun,Junxu Liu,Xiaokai Mao,Haocheng Xia,Jinfei Liu,Kui Ren,Haibo Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出基于边干预的DAG-SHAP方法，将特征边作为归因对象，解决现有特征归因方法难以同时捕捉特征外部性和外生影响的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Shapley值的特征归因方法在面对复杂特征交互和因果结构时，采用以节点为中心的视角，仅归因于单个特征，无法同时捕捉特征的外部性（externality）和外生影响（exogenous influence），导致解释不合理。

Method: 提出DAG-SHAP方法，将每条特征边（feature edge）作为独立的归因对象，基于边干预（edge intervention）进行Shapley值计算，并引入高效近似计算方法。

Result: 在真实和合成数据集上的大量实验验证了DAG-SHAP的有效性。

Conclusion: DAG-SHAP通过边级归因克服了节点级归因的局限性，能同时捕捉特征的外部性和外生贡献，提供了更合理的特征归因解释。

Abstract: Shapley value-based feature attribution methods face challenges in scenarios involving complex feature interactions and causal relationships, even when a causal structure is provided. Existing methods typically adopt a node-centric view, attributing importance solely to individual features. Consequently, they often fail to simultaneously capture the externality and exogenous influence of features, leading to unreasonable interpretations. To overcome these limitations, we propose a novel feature attribution method called DAG-SHAP, which is based on edge intervention. DAG-SHAP treats each feature edge as an individual attribution object, ensuring that both externality and exogenous contributions of features are appropriately captured. Additionally, we introduce an approximation method for efficiently computing DAG-SHAP. Extensive experiments on both real and synthetic datasets validate the effectiveness of DAG-SHAP. Our code is available at https://github.com/ZJU-DIVER/DAG-SHAP.

</details>


### [98] [A Formal Framework for Declarative Agentic AI in Business Process Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15291)
*Mohammad Azarijafari,Luisa Mich,Michele Missikoff*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AGO方法论（Agent-Goal-Object），基于集合论和数学逻辑为智能体驱动的业务流程分析建立形式化框架，通过构建业务流程知识库支持结构化查询、增量更新和工作流自动生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体AI虽为业务流程自动化带来新机遇，但需要精确的形式化定义来规范BP实体及其交互，现有方法缺乏严格的数学基础。

Method: 提出AGO方法论，从Agent（行动者）、Goal（目标）、Object（对象）三个维度建模；基于集合论和数学逻辑形式化定义实体类型及其交互；构建业务流程知识库（BPKB）。

Result: AGO框架支持结构化查询、增量更新、BP工作流自动生成，并保证推导路径的可靠性和完备性。

Conclusion: AGO形式化框架为智能体驱动的业务流程分析提供了严格的数学基础，能够有效支撑自动化流程生成与动态适应。

Abstract: Agentic AI opens new opportunities for automating Business Process (BP), enabling autonomous decision-making and dynamic adaptation. However, realising this potential requires BP entities and their interactions to be defined with formal precision. This paper presents a formal framework for Agentic BP analysis through the AGO methodology. AGO captures the modelling perspective in terms of who is acting (Agents), why it is carried out (Goals), and what the relevant entities are (Objects). Grounded in set theory and mathematical logic, we formally define the AGO entity types and their interactions, organising all definitions into a BP Knowledge Base (BPKB). The resulting BPKB supports structured querying, incremental updates, and automatic generation of BP workflows, while ensuring soundness and completeness of the derived paths.

</details>


### [99] [CODA-BENCH: Can Code Agents Handle Data-Intensive Tasks?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15300)
*Yuxin Zhang,Ju Fan,Meihao Fan,Shaolei Zhang,Xiaoyong Du*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CODA-BENCH 是一个首个联合评估代码与数据智能的基准测试，基于 Kaggle 生态系统构建了包含数百个数据集的数据密集型 Linux 沙箱环境，涵盖 31 个社区的 1,009 个任务（平均每个任务 980 个文件）。评估表明，即使最先进的智能体在整合数据发现与代码执行方面成功率仅为 61.1%，揭示了数据密集型任务中当前智能体能力的显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试通常孤立评估代码或数据能力，与真实开发场景（同时涉及复杂代码和大规模数据/文件系统）存在明显差距。需要一个新的基准来弥合这一鸿沟，联合评估数据密集型环境中的代码与数据智能。

Method: 基于 Kaggle 生态系统构建数据密集型 Linux 沙箱，包含数百个数据集；智能体需主动探索复杂文件层次结构以识别相关资源，并生成代码完成数据驱动的分析任务。共构建 1,009 个任务，覆盖 31 个社区，每个任务环境平均包含 980 个文件以模拟真实数据规模与噪声。

Result: 对先进智能体的评估显示，即使顶尖系统在整合数据发现与代码执行方面成功率仅为 61.1%，表明存在显著能力差距。

Conclusion: 当前智能体在数据密集型任务中难以有效整合数据发现与代码执行，指出了有前景的未来研究方向。

Abstract: Advanced agents are increasingly demonstrating the potential to operate as autonomous engineers, creating a growing demand for evaluation benchmarks that capture the complexity of real-world development. Such environments typically involve both complex code and large-scale data (i.e., file system). However, existing benchmarks usually evaluate code-centric or data-centric capabilities in isolation, leaving a clear gap with real development scenarios. In this paper, we bridge this gap by introducing CODA-BENCH, the first benchmark to jointly evaluate code and data intelligence in a data-intensive environment. We construct a data-intensive Linux sandbox based on the Kaggle ecosystem (containing hundreds of datasets), where agents must actively explore complex file hierarchies to identify relevant resources and generate code for data-driven analytical tasks. CODA-BENCH comprises 1,009 tasks spanning 31 communities, with each task environment containing an average of 980 files, simulating realistic data scale and noise. Evaluations of advanced agents reveal that even top-performing systems struggle to effectively integrate data discovery with code execution, achieving a success rate of only 61.1%. These results highlight a substantial gap in current agentic capabilities for data-intensive tasks and point to promising directions for future research.

</details>


### [100] [Forced Deferral: Manipulating Routing Decisions in Multimodal LLM Cascades](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15308)
*Zhongye Liu,Yaopei Zeng,Yurui Chang,Lu Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出强制延迟攻击（FDA），通过对抗性图像触发器降低弱模型置信度，使多模态大模型级联系统将查询路由到强模型，从而消耗更多计算资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLM级联系统通过弱模型置信度控制计算分配，但这一机制存在安全漏洞——攻击者可操纵置信度迫使查询被路由到强模型，导致不必要的计算开销。

Method: FDA学习一个通用边框触发器，通过温度展平（temperature-flattened）目标函数优化，将弱模型在触发输入上的token分布推向基于其干净响应构建的、更不集中的目标分布。

Result: 在多个数据集、模型系列和延迟指标上，FDA一致地增加了强模型路由比例，性能优于图像扰动和提示注入基线。

Conclusion: MLLM级联系统容易受到操纵计算分配的攻击，攻击者可在不影响答案正确性的情况下强制消耗强模型资源。

Abstract: While multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have shown strong visual reasoning abilities, serving a large model for every query is computationally expensive. MLLM cascades mitigate this cost by first querying a weak but cheaper model and deferring to a strong model when the weak model's output is unconfident. However, since the weak model's confidence directly controls compute allocation, these systems expose a new attack surface: an adversary can manipulate confidence so that their queries are consistently deferred to the strong model. Motivated by this vulnerability, we introduce the Forced Deferral Attack (FDA), an adversarial image attack that lowers the weak model's confidence and causes cascades to route queries to the strong model. FDA learns a universal border trigger by optimizing a temperature-flattened objective. This objective pushes the weak model's token distribution on triggered inputs toward less concentrated targets constructed from its clean responses. Across datasets, model families, and deferral metrics, FDA consistently increases strong-model routing while outperforming image-perturbation and prompt-injection baselines. These results show that MLLM cascades are vulnerable to attacks that manipulate compute allocation, forcing unintended strong-model usage without directly targeting answer correctness.

</details>


### [101] [ChatPlanner: A Large Language Model Framework for Personalized Public Transit Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15315)
*Tingting Yang,Chenhao Xue,Jun Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ChatPlanner框架，利用大语言模型（LLMs）结合检索增强生成（RAG）技术，从自然语言查询中提取路由参数和用户偏好，并将其整合到公共交通路由算法的目标函数中，实现个性化路线规划。实验表明，该框架能可靠生成可行方案，微调学习通用偏好模式，RAG提供查询特定上下文信息，两者结合在路由信息提取和偏好解释方面表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公共交通系统中的个性化路线规划因难以捕获和整合多样化的用户偏好而面临挑战，现有路由规划器无法充分理解用户自然语言表达的细微偏好差异。

Method: 采用微调LLMs结合RAG技术，从自然语言查询中提取路由参数并解释用户偏好，将偏好整合到路由算法目标函数中；设计了包含8种角色和5种上下文的偏好感知数据集。

Result: ChatPlanner能可靠生成可行方案；微调强制输出结构并学习通用偏好模式，RAG提供查询特定上下文解决模糊表达并校准连续分数，两者结合在路由信息提取和用户偏好解释上准确率最高；案例研究表明其能发现现有规划器忽略的更有价值的路线方案。

Conclusion: 建立了将自然语言理解整合到交通优化的新范式，实现了基于用户偏好的个性化公共交通路线规划。

Abstract: Personalized public transit routing in public transit systems remains challenging due to the difficulty of capturing and integrating diverse user preferences into routing algorithms. This paper presents ChatPlanner, a novel framework that leverages Large Language Models (LLMs) to enable preference aware public transit routing. Our approach employs fine-tuned LLMs with Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) to extract routing parameters and interpret nuanced user preferences from natural language queries, subsequently integrating these preferences into the objective function of a public transit routing algorithm. This study designs preference aware datasets incorporating eight personas and five contexts to establish scoring standards for both fine-tuning and RAG. This work conducted three experiments to validate the solutions' feasibility, extraction of routing information and preferences, and solution set quality and completeness. Results demonstrate that ChatPlanner generates feasible solutions reliably. Fine-tuning enforces the required output structure and learns general preference patterns, while RAG provides query-specific context to resolve imprecise or conversational expressions and calibrate continuous scores. The combination of both achieves the highest accuracy in routing information extraction and user preference interpretation. Results based on selected case studies show that by capturing user preferences, ChatPlanner identifies valuable solutions across different dimensions that existing route planners overlook, generating more valuable route alternatives. This research establishes a new paradigm for integrating natural language understanding into transportation optimization.

</details>


### [102] [APEX: Adaptive Principle EXtraction A Three-Layer Self-Evolution Framework for Production AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15363)
*Ya-Chuan Chen,Tien-Jen Lai,Hsiang-Wei Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: APEX 是一个三维协同进化框架，同时优化AI代理的提示词（L1）、行为原则（L2）和工作流拓扑（L3），相比仅优化提示词的单轴方法（Self-Harness），在真实边缘AI集群任务上实现了+90%的健康分数提升，仅需4次LLM调用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自我改进框架（如Self-Harness）仅优化单一维度（提示词），忽略了行为原则和工作流拓扑的协同进化潜力，导致性能提升受限。

Method: 提出APEX三层协同进化框架：L1通过失败模式修补优化提示词；L2通过成功轨迹蒸馏提取行为原则；L3通过结构适应度选择进化工作流拓扑。在Joe代理系统上基于114个真实任务轨迹和18天数据验证。

Result: APEX健康分数达0.570（较基线0.300提升+90%），蒸馏出6个可复用原则，工作流拓扑得分0.900（+20%），仅需4次LLM调用（约270秒）。

Conclusion: 多维协同进化显著优于单轴提示词优化，以极低计算成本实现大幅性能提升，为AI代理自我改进提供了更全面的范式。

Abstract: Self-improvement in AI agents has emerged as a key research frontier: systems that modify their own prompts, workflows, and decision rules based on accumulated operational experience. The state-of-the-art Self-Harness framework [1] achieves 14--21% improvement on Terminal-Bench-2.0 by mining failure clusters and patching the agent harness. However, Self-Harness optimises only one dimension -- the prompt harness -- leaving behavioural principles and workflow topology unchanged. We propose APEX (Adaptive Principle EXtraction), a three-layer co-evolution framework that simultaneously evolves: (L1) the harness via failure-mode patching, (L2) behavioural principles via success-trace distillation [2], and (L3) the agent workflow topology via structural fitness-based selection [6]. We implement APEX on Joe [13], a production-grade super AI Agent built on NVIDIA Nemotron and designed as an Edge AI Agent Factory for the NVIDIA Agent Challenge 2026, managing a 15-node compute fleet using 114 real task traces collected over 18 days. APEX achieves an APEX Health Score of 0.570 (+90% vs. baseline 0.300) in a single evolutionary run, distilling 6 novel reusable principles and selecting a research-first workflow topology scoring 0.900 (+20%). Our results demonstrate that multi-dimensional co-evolution substantially outperforms single-axis harness optimisation, at a cost of only 4 LLM calls (~270 s) on a local qwen2.5-coder:32b instance.

</details>


### [103] [Do we have the knowledge we need? Rethinking human-AI decision-making in corporations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15575)
*Anne S. R. Marx,Ricardo M. Avelino,Torbjørn Netland,Mennatallah El-Assady*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了组织知识碎片化对AI系统部署的挑战，提出了一个根据任务属性和知识可用性来分配人类与AI代理权责的框架，并通过两个制造业案例（视觉质检和工厂选址）进行了说明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI系统越来越多地被赋予决策角色，它们需要访问分散在各类软件、隐性知识和文档中的组织知识。现有知识管理方式主要针对人类设计，如何让知识同时服务于人类和未来AI系统，以及如何在不同风险和不确定性水平的任务中分配人类与AI的代理权，是亟待解决的问题。

Method: 采用立场论文的形式，首先描述了组织知识的演化过程，然后提出了一个将任务属性和知识可用性映射到推荐代理权分配与控制机制的框架，并通过两个制造业任务（视觉质量检测和工厂选址）进行应用说明。

Result: 提出了一个可操作的框架，能够根据任务的风险、不确定性以及知识的可用性，推荐不同的代理权分配方案（人类主导、AI主导、混合协作）和相应的控制机制。

Conclusion: 该框架为组织在部署AI系统时如何管理知识和分配决策权提供了指导，未来研究可进一步探索框架在不同行业和更复杂任务中的适用性与扩展性。

Abstract: Organizational knowledge is fragmented across a variety of software systems, tacit expertise, and manual documents that have traditionally been designed for human consumption. As AI systems are increasingly deployed and granted decision-making roles, they require access to this knowledge. This raises two questions: how should organizations store and maintain knowledge so that it remains accessible to both humans and future AI systems, and how should agency be allocated between humans and AI across tasks with different risks and levels of uncertainty? In this position paper, we describe how organizational knowledge evolves and contribute a framework that maps task attributes and knowledge availability to recommended agency allocations and control mechanisms. We illustrate the applicability of the framework on two different manufacturing tasks: a routine operation (visual quality inspection) and a one-off strategic decision (factory location), and conclude with opportunities for future research.

</details>


### [104] [S1-DeepResearch: Beyond Search, Toward Real-World Long-Horizon Research Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15367)
*Yao Dong,Xinglin Xiao,Liwei Dong,Xinlong Jin,Zhengbo Li,Heng Zhang,Duyun Wang,Nan Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向深度研究智能体的统一轨迹构建范式，通过结合闭端问答与开放探索、图基任务构建、轨迹生成与多维验证，合成了高质量智能体轨迹。基于该数据训练的S1-DeepResearch-32B在20个基准测试中达到开源模型最优性能，接近领先闭源模型水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有训练数据集以搜索为中心，主要覆盖信息检索和闭端问答，缺乏对证据整合、知识综合、规划、文件理解和结构化报告生成等深度研究关键能力的覆盖。

Method: 提出统一的轨迹构建范式，包含三个核心组件：图基任务构建（graph-grounded task formulation）、智能体轨迹展开（agentic trajectory rollout）、多维轨迹验证（multi-dimensional trajectory verification）。该方法结合闭端问答与开放探索，可规模化合成高质量智能体轨迹。

Result: S1-DeepResearch-32B在20个基准测试（涵盖复杂推理、指令遵循、报告生成、文件理解、技能使用五个维度）上达到开源模型中同等规模的最优性能，在多个挑战性深度研究基准上接近领先闭源前沿模型。

Conclusion: 联合建模信息获取、知识综合与规划导向的智能体行为对于构建有效的深度研究智能体至关重要。

Abstract: Deep research agents aim to solve complex knowledge-intensive tasks through long-horizon planning, evidence gathering, reasoning, and report generation. While recent progress in search agents has demonstrated strong capabilities in information retrieval and answer verification, most existing training datasets remain search-centric, focusing primarily on closed-ended question answering and information localization. As a result, they mainly train information-seeking behavior while providing limited coverage of key deep research capabilities, including evidence integration, knowledge synthesis, planning, file understanding, and structured report generation. In this work, we propose a unified trajectory construction paradigm for deep research agents that combines closed-ended QA and open-ended exploration. The proposed framework consists of graph-grounded task formulation, agentic trajectory rollout, and multi-dimensional trajectory verification, enabling scalable synthesis of high-quality agentic trajectories spanning long-chain complex reasoning, deep research instruction following, report writing, file understanding and generation, and skills usage. Compared with existing search-oriented datasets, our synthesized trajectories place greater emphasis on knowledge synthesis, complex reasoning, and planning. S1-DeepResearch-32B achieves state-of-the-art performance among open-source models of comparable scale across 20 benchmarks spanning five capability dimensions, including complex reasoning, instruction following, report generation, file understanding, and skills usage. On several challenging deep research benchmarks, it approaches the performance of leading proprietary frontier models. These results highlight the importance of jointly modeling information acquisition, knowledge synthesis, and planning-oriented agent behaviors for building effective deep research agents.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [105] [Bayesian Optimization for Learning Nonlinear MPC in Autonomous Agent Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14763)
*Lorenzo Ortolani,Gabriel Voss,Gabriele Beltrami,Francesco Dorati,Tommaso Felice Banfi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合滚动时域规划与非线性MPC的无地图自主导航框架，通过离线贝叶斯优化自动调参，在四足机器人上实现90%导航成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态未知环境下的实时自主导航对移动机器人仍是基础性挑战，现有方法依赖地图构建且参数敏感，需要一种鲁棒且无需人工调参的导航框架。

Method: 1) 基于LiDAR的高斯占据表示构建；2) 采用A*搜索生成无碰撞轨迹的滚动时域规划；3) 使用CasADi/IPOPT实现非线性MPC轨迹跟踪，含sigmoid障碍屏障函数；4) 离线TPE贝叶斯优化控制器参数；5) 高斯过程代理模型分析参数敏感性。

Result: 仿真调参有效迁移至硬件，无需额外调参；部署导航成功率高达90.0%；模拟环境中评估指标平均提升38.9%。

Conclusion: 提出的机器人无关框架在四足机器人上验证了仿真到实物的参数迁移能力，实现了高成功率实时导航，且对参数敏感性问题具有较好鲁棒性。

Abstract: Real-time autonomous navigation in dynamic, unknown environments remains a fundamental challenge for mobile robotics. We propose a map-free framework that tightly integrates reactive rolling-horizon planning with nonlinear Model Predictive Control (MPC). At each control cycle, a LiDAR-based Gaussian occupancy representation is constructed and used to generate collision-free trajectories via A* search, which are then tracked by a CasADi/IPOPT MPC formulation incorporating a smooth sigmoid obstacle barrier. To improve robustness to parameter sensitivity, we adopt an offline Bayesian optimization scheme based on Tree-structured Parzen Estimators (TPE), which identifies near-optimal controller parameters with respect to a composite navigation objective. In addition, a Gaussian Process surrogate is used to analyze parameter sensitivity and provide insight into the optimization landscape.
  The proposed framework is robot-agnostic and is evaluated on the Unitree Go2 quadruped in simulation using Gazebo, followed by deployment on the physical robot. Experimental results show that parameters tuned in simulation transfer effectively to hardware, maintaining comparable performance without additional tuning. The full system achieves up to a 90.0\% navigation success rate when deployed, along with a 38.9\% average improvement in the evaluation metrics across simulated environments.

</details>


### [106] [Synthetic-to-Real Pipeline for Safe Landing Zone Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14767)
*Shrikant Banerjee,Reza Faieghi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套面向无人机自主着陆的感知与数据生成管线，通过程序化合成数据引擎生成逼真城市环境并自动化语义标注，基于OneFormer架构在纯合成数据上微调实现语义分割，结合欧氏距离变换与动态推理确定最大内接安全着陆区域，在UAVid数据集上验证了语义分割性能，并在真实无人机 footage 上证明了零样本迁移能力，消除了对人工标注的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机向高自主性过渡时，需要在非协作、非结构化环境中实现无辅助自主着陆，但高保真语义分辨能力受限于带标注航拍数据集的稀缺性，人工标注成本高且难以规模化。现有方法在模拟到真实的迁移中表现不佳，亟需一种无需人工标注即可实现鲁棒感知与安全着陆区检测的方案。

Method: 提出完整的感知与数据生成管线：(1) 程序化合成数据引擎，通过域随机化生成逼真城市环境并自动输出语义标注；(2) 基于Transformer的OneFormer架构，利用多头自注意力机制捕捉全局上下文，仅在合成数据上微调；(3) 确定性着陆模块，采用欧氏距离变换(EDT)和动态推理逻辑，从分割结果中识别最大内接安全着陆区域，并保持严格的障碍物安全缓冲区。

Result: 在UAVid数据集上的定量基准测试中取得了鲁棒的语义分割性能；在真实无人机视频上的定性验证表明，系统能够在未见过的环境中识别无碰撞安全着陆点，证明了零样本迁移的有效性。

Conclusion: 高保真程序化仿真能够消除对人工标注的依赖，为无人机自主回收提供鲁棒且可在边缘部署的态势感知能力，有效弥合了模拟到现实的差距。

Abstract: As Uncrewed Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) transition toward higher levels of autonomy, the ability to perform unassisted recovery in non-cooperative, unstructured environments becomes critical. Achieving safe autonomous landing requires high-fidelity semantic resolution to distinguish navigable terrain from hazardous obstacles, yet development is often hindered by the scarcity of annotated aerial datasets. This work proposes a comprehensive perception and data generation pipeline designed to bridge the sim-to-real gap for autonomous landing tasks. We introduce a procedural synthetic data engine that generates photorealistic urban environments with automated semantic annotations through domain randomization. A Transformer-based OneFormer architecture is fine-tuned exclusively on this synthetic data, leveraging multi-head self-attention mechanisms for global context resolution. To ensure operational safety, a deterministic landing module utilizes a Euclidean Distance Transform (EDT) and dynamic inference logic to identify the largest inscribed safe landing zones while maintaining strict clearance buffers around obstacles. Quantitative benchmarking against the UAVid dataset demonstrates robust semantic segmentation performance, while qualitative validation on real-world UAV footage confirms the system's ability to identify collision-free landing sites in unseen environments. Our results highlight the potential of high-fidelity procedural simulation to eliminate the need for manual annotation while providing robust, edge-deployable situational awareness for autonomous UAV recovery.

</details>


### [107] [Deep Learning-Based Lunar Crater Terrain Relative Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14776)
*Batu Candan,Simone Servadio*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合深度学习陨石坑检测器和扩展卡尔曼滤波的地形相对导航算法，能在初始位置偏差达5公里的情况下将导航误差降低至数百米，适用于未来月球着陆任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未来月球着陆任务中，自主飞行器在危险且地形特征稀疏的环境下需要精确的位置估计，传统方法难以满足需求。

Method: 使用针对NASA陨石坑检测挑战赛设计的深度学习检测器从单目图像中分析陨石坑特征，通过匈牙利分配法和基于共识的离群点去除方法进行全球数据库匹配，结合高度辅助信息在月心月固参考系中构建扩展卡尔曼滤波进行位姿估计。

Result: 在初始位置偏差高达5公里的情况下，TRN算法仍能将导航误差降低至数百米。

Conclusion: 所提TRN算法能有效恢复大范围初始位置误差，但需要保持图像分辨率与检测器训练集分布的一致性以维持陨石坑特征对应关系。

Abstract: Accurate position estimation is crucial for the successful implementation of future lunar landings using autonomous vehicles, especially in dangerous environments with sparse terrain features. In this paper, we propose a terrain relative navigation (TRN) algorithm combining our deep-learning crater detector, which was designed specifically for the NASA Crater Detection Challenge problem, and an Extended Kalman Filter (EKF). Our detector analyzes crater features from the monocular images acquired from orbit, and their matches with craters from a global database are identified via a Hungarian assignment approach followed by the consensus-based outliers removal method. The estimated measurements are then used to refine an EKF, where spacecraft pose estimation in the Lunar-Centered Lunar-Fixed (LCLF) frame of reference, augmented with altitude aiding information, constrains radial drift. The simulation results indicate that even if the spacecraft is off from its actual location up to 5 km, TRN could recover from this situation, achieving navigation error reduction to a few hundred meters. It should be noted that in order to maintain crater feature correspondences, it is important to match the image resolution and the scales within the scene to the detector training set distribution.

</details>


### [108] [Computing Smooth Geodesics under Two-Sided Curvature Bounds with Applications to Robotics and Image Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14794)
*Da Chen,Zhenjiang Li,Jean-Marie Mirebeau,Xuecheng Tai,Jinglin Zhang,Wei Zhang,Laurent D. Cohen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种在Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman偏微分方程框架下开发的曲率有界测地线模型，通过施加曲率上下界约束来跟踪光滑且曲率受限的最小路径，并给出了哈密顿量和HJB PDE的离散化方案，在机器人路径规划和图像曲线结构跟踪中展示了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 曲率作为平面曲线二阶正则化项，与光滑性、刚性和弹性等几何属性紧密相关；然而现有方法难以处理曲率受任意上下界约束的最小路径跟踪问题，需要更强的几何控制工具。

Method: 在HJB PDE框架下构建曲率有界测地线模型，通过施加曲率范围约束实现几何控制；提出针对哈密顿量和HJB PDE的离散化方案，以高效求解数值解。

Result: 数值实验表明，该模型在机器人路径规划和图像曲线结构跟踪任务中能够找到令人满意的路径，是一种强大且鲁棒的工具。

Conclusion: 提出的曲率有界测地线模型为计算曲率约束下的二阶最小路径提供了有效框架，兼具理论严谨性与实际应用价值。

Abstract: Curvature of planar curves serves as a key regularization term for computing second-order minimal paths, due to its tight relevance to desirable geometric properties such as smoothness, rigidity, and elasticity. In this paper, we tackle a more challenging problem in computational physics and geometry problem: tracking minimal paths whose curvature is constrained by arbitrary upper and lower bounds. For that purpose, we propose a new curvature-bounded geodesic model, developed under the Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman (HJB) partial differential equation (PDE) framework. It provides strong geometric control over minimal paths by enforcing curvature range constraints, whose paths are smooth and of bounded curvature limitation. We also present a discretization scheme for the Hamiltonian and the HJB PDE incorporating curvature bounds, allowing efficient solver for estimating numerical solutions to the model. Finally, we illustrate the capability of the proposed curvature-bounded geodesic model in applications of robot path planning and curvilinear structures tracking from images. Numerical experiments demonstrate that the proposed curvature-bounded geodesic model serves as a powerful and robust tool for finding satisfactory paths.

</details>


### [109] [TacStyle: Personalizing Tactile Robot Policies using Structured Behavior Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14862)
*Kevin Robledo,Matías I. Torres Galaz,Kumar Dixhant Rai,Shelly Sara Ulman,Tasmia Tasrin,Heramb Nemlekar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出通过语言推理偏好行为而非直接生成，学习结构化潜在空间来组织用户偏好，并使用基础模型解析该空间以选择产生期望行为的参数，从而实现机器人行为对用户偏好的精细适配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言条件策略需要大量详细标签来指定任务执行方式，且难以对连续行为范围进行精细控制（如精确传达所需力度），因此需要一种更高效的方法来适配用户偏好。

Method: 首先学习一个结构化潜在表示，根据轨迹差异组织用户偏好；然后给定偏好提示时，使用基础模型（foundation model）解析该潜在空间，选择能产生期望行为的参数值。

Result: 仿真和真实实验表明，从直观结构化的潜在空间中选择机器人行为，能比语言条件策略更精确地适配用户偏好，同时所需偏好标签显著更少。

Conclusion: 所提方法通过语言推理偏好而非直接生成，有效解决了精细行为控制与标签效率之间的矛盾，为机器人个性化行为适配提供了新范式。

Abstract: Robotic systems that assist humans should be capable of adapting their behaviors to individual user preferences. For instance, users may want a robot arm to adjust the amount of force it applies while folding their laundry or cleaning furniture. Natural language provides an intuitive way for humans to communicate such preferences. Recent progress in language-conditioned robot policies has shown that robots can successfully use language prompts to determine what task to perform. However, extending the same approach to realize how the task should be performed requires detailed labels describing the preferences or styles of trajectories in the task data. Not only is collecting such annotations challenging, but conditioning directly on these labels may also fail to provide fine-grained control over a continuous range of behaviors. For example, it can be difficult to convey the exact force that a robot must apply through abstract instructions like "apply a bit more pressure than before". Therefore, in this work, we propose using language to reason over preferred behaviors instead of directly generating them. We first learn a structured latent representation that organizes user preferences according to differences in the corresponding trajectories. Then, given a preference prompt, we use a foundation model to interpret this latent space and choose a value that produces the desired behavior. Through both simulation and real-world experiments, we show that selecting robot behaviors from an intuitively structured latent space enables more precise adaptation to user preferences while requiring significantly fewer preference labels than language-conditioned policies.

</details>


### [110] [VANDERER: Map-Free Exploration using Future-Aware and Visual-Curiosity-Guided Diffusion Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14879)
*Venkata Naren Devarakonda,Raktim Gautam Goswami,Prashanth Krishnamurthy,Farshad Khorrami*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VANDERER是一个利用视觉好奇心模块（VCM）引导预训练扩散策略的探索框架，仅依赖单目图像数据，在传感器受限条件下实现高效环境探索，相比NoMaD平均多探索13.4%的区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法依赖占用地图生成与未探索区域序列优化，但在单目相机等传感器受限条件下难以生成准确占用地图，因此需要一种不依赖占用地图的探索策略。

Method: 提出VANDERER框架，包含视觉好奇心模块（VCM），通过导航世界模型预测动作结果并计算好奇心代价，利用该代价引导扩散过程生成最大化探索的动作。

Result: 在多种模拟环境中一致优于基线方法，相比NoMaD平均多探索13.4%的区域；发现户外环境中视觉好奇心与几何好奇心之间存在直接相关性。

Conclusion: VANDERER能够有效利用视觉与几何好奇心之间的关系，在传感器受限条件下实现高效探索。

Abstract: Mobile agents require efficient exploration strategies to map unseen environments and autonomously plan tasks. Traditional methods rely on generating occupancy maps and optimizing the sequence in which unexplored regions are visited. However, in sensor-constrained settings, such as those limited to monocular cameras, generating accurate occupancy maps is challenging. To address this, we propose VANDERER, an exploration framework that leverages a Visual Curiosity Module (VCM) to guide pre-trained diffusion policies using only monocular image data. This curiosity module predicts the outcomes of proposed actions via a navigation world model and evaluates them through a curiosity cost. The cost then guides the diffusion process toward generating actions that maximize exploration. Evaluated across diverse simulated environments, VANDERER consistently outperforms established baselines, exploring an average of 13.4% more area than NoMaD. Our results reveal a direct correlation between visual and geometric curiosity in outdoor environments, demonstrating that VANDERER can effectively leverage this relationship for efficient exploration using sensor-constrained agents.

</details>


### [111] [DynaHMRC: Decentralized Heterogeneous Multi-Robot Collaboration for Dynamic Tasks with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14882)
*Wenhao Yu,Yu'ang Xie,Yifan Duan,Jie Peng,Guanting Ye,Ka-Veng Yuen,Yanyong Zhang,Jianmin Ji*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DynaHMRC是一个去中心化框架，将每个机器人建模为角色感知的LLM代理，通过四阶段闭环流程（自描述、任务分配与领导竞标、领导者选举、反思执行）解决集中式LLM调度器在异构多机器人长时域任务中的扩展性、动态适应性及领域数据稀缺问题，在更少动作和通信步骤下实现更高成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的多机器人协调面临三个未充分探索的挑战：(1) 集中式LLM调度器在团队规模和环境复杂度增加时扩展性差，长上下文近似会降低推理质量；(2) 现有任务公式未充分考虑动态环境，而鲁棒适应演化任务条件对实际部署至关重要；(3) 领域特定数据稀缺限制了专业机器人推理能力，通用模型在专家任务中效率低下。

Method: 提出DynaHMRC框架：(1) 去中心化架构，每个机器人作为角色感知的LLM代理，缓解单模型上下文瓶颈；(2) 四阶段闭环协作流程：自描述、任务分配与领导竞标、领导者选举、反思执行，由可执行机器人接口支持；(3) 构建包含三个任务族、四种动态变化和六种团队配置的基准用于动态任务建模系统研究；(4) 进行实证分析指导领域特定专家数据集构建，微调预训练LLM以提升专业能力。

Result: DynaHMRC在更少的动作和通信步骤下实现了比强基线更高的成功率，并在评估设置内随团队规模增长展现出有前景的可扩展性趋势。

Conclusion: 去中心化的角色感知LLM代理框架能有效解决集中式调度在多机器人长时域任务中的扩展性、动态适应性和领域迁移问题，为异构多机器人系统的实际部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) provide robots with richer task understanding and adaptability, making them promising for coordinating heterogeneous multi-robot systems in long-horizon tasks. Despite this potential, several challenges remain underexplored: (1) Centralized LLM schedulers scale poorly as team size and environmental complexity increase. A single model must process excessive contextual information, and long-context approximation may degrade reasoning quality; (2) Existing task formulations insufficiently consider dynamic settings, while robust adaptation to evolving task conditions is essential for real-world deployment; (3) Domain-specific data scarcity limits specialized robotic reasoning, making proprietary general-purpose models inefficient for expert tasks. To address these limitations, we propose DynaHMRC, a decentralized framework in which each robot acts as a role-aware LLM agent. This design mitigates the single-model context bottleneck and supports flexible collaboration across heterogeneous team configurations. DynaHMRC organizes collaboration as a four-stage closed-loop process: self-description, task allocation with leadership bidding, leader election, and reflective execution, supported by executable robot interfaces. We further develop a benchmark covering three task families, four dynamic variations, and six team configurations to systematically study dynamic task modeling. In addition, we conduct an empirical analysis to guide the construction of domain-specific expert datasets and fine-tune pretrained LLMs to improve specialized competence. Experiments show that DynaHMRC achieves higher success rates than strong baselines with fewer action and communication steps, while demonstrating promising scalability trends as team size grows within the evaluated settings.

</details>


### [112] [Multimodal Physiological Assessment of Contact-Rich Physical Human-Robot Interaction Under Varying Environmental Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14969)
*Yanyi Chen,Xi Wang,Min Deng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文通过多模态生理信号实验发现，操作者在接触式人机交互任务中会通过增加生理努力（如皮肤电导水平升高）来补偿热不适，从而维持稳定的任务表现，揭示了传统任务评价指标所忽略的隐性生理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统任务中心评估忽视了真实环境中温度、噪声、光照等压力源对操作者造成的生理负担，需要揭示这些隐性生理成本。

Method: 在18种温度、噪声、光照组合条件下，同步记录EDA、sEMG、眼动数据和主观舒适度评分，结合执行数据进行多模态分析。

Result: 任务表现跨条件稳定；自主神经负荷（由SCL指示）随温度升高而增加；身体和认知负荷不受影响；主观舒适度与追踪误差/完成时间无显著关联。

Conclusion: 操作者通过增加生理努力补偿热不适以维持表现，这启发开发利用实时生理指标降低操作者负荷的生理感知控制架构。

Abstract: Physical human-robot interaction (pHRI) in real-world settings exposes operators to fluctuating environmental conditions during contact-rich tasks. Traditional task-centric evaluations overlook the physiological burdens imposed by these stressors. Therefore, we conducted a multimodal empirical study involving contact-rich tracing tasks under 18 distinct combinations of temperature, acoustic noise, and illuminance. Synchronously, we recorded electrodermal activity (EDA), surface electromyography (sEMG), eye-tracking data, and subjective environmental comfort ratings. Evaluating these physiological signals alongside execution data revealed hidden physiological costs not captured by objective performance. The results revealed that task performance remained stable across all environmental conditions. Autonomic workload, indexed by tonic skin conductance level (SCL), increased with temperature, while physical and cognitive workload were unaffected. Perceived environmental comfort showed no significant association with tracing error or completion time. These findings reveal a compensatory mechanism where operators maintain consistent performance by increasing their physiological effort to suppress thermal discomfort. Such insight motivates the development of physiology-aware control architectures that leverage real-time physiological metrics to reduce operator workload in unstructured environments.

</details>


### [113] [Inference-time Policy Steering via Vision and Touch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14981)
*Yilin Wu,Zilin Si,Zeynep Temel,Oliver Kroemer,Andrea Bajcsy*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ViTaL是一种视觉-触觉推理时引导框架，通过双层优化实现机器人灵巧操作：高层视觉采样验证选择行为模式，低层触觉引导扩散编辑优化局部接触，在接触丰富的操作任务中成功率比基础策略提升51%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理时引导方法仅依赖视觉观测，但对于接触丰富的操作任务，视觉信息不足以捕捉接触力等细微局部交互，需要引入触觉模态实现多模态引导。

Method: ViTaL框架将多模态引导形式化为双层优化问题：高层通过视觉采样-验证进行长视野模式选择；低层通过触觉引导的扩散编辑在短视野内优化动作序列以满足局部接触需求。此外，学习视觉-触觉潜在世界模型，采用语义对齐的视觉和触觉验证器，包括新颖的文本条件触觉奖励。

Result: 在三个真实世界的接触丰富操作任务中，ViTaL将成功率提升51%（相比基础策略），比单模态引导提升至少33%，比朴素多模态融合提升至少20%。

Conclusion: 视觉与触觉的多模态融合推理时引导策略能有效提升接触丰富操作任务的性能，验证了触觉模态在局部接触精调中的关键作用。

Abstract: Inference-time steering adapts pre-trained generative robot policies during deployment by verifying candidate actions before execution. While prior methods typically perform this verification only with visual observations, vision alone is often insufficient for contact-rich manipulation, where success depends on both global task progress and subtle local interactions such as contact force. We introduce ViTaL, a visuo-tactile inference-time steering framework that formulates multimodal guidance as a bi-level optimization problem. At the high level, visual sampling-and-verification performs long-horizon mode selection, deciding what behavior the robot should execute. At the low level, tactile-guided diffusion editing refines the selected action sequence over a shorter horizon to satisfy local contact requirements. To support outcome-based steering, ViTaL learns a visuo-tactile latent world model and employs semantically aligned visual and tactile verifiers, including a novel text-conditioned tactile reward that scores predicted tactile futures directly in latent space. Across three real-world contact-rich manipulation tasks, ViTaL improves overall success by 51% over the base policy, outperforms unimodal steering by at least 33%, and exceeds naive multimodal fusion by at least 20%. Website: https://yilin-wu98.github.io/vital_website.

</details>


### [114] [LV-Calib: LiDAR-Camera Extrinsic Calibration with Boundary-Response Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15010)
*Sheng Hong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LV-Calib提出了一种使用可打印平面靶标进行LiDAR-相机外参标定和LiDAR边界响应校准的统一框架，通过圆形反射率边界提供可观测的结构特征点，并利用强度与几何约束迭代优化3D特征点，实现了亚像素重投影精度和毫米级LiDAR特征一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LiDAR-相机标定方法在利用平面靶标时，常因LiDAR光束足印与黑白反射率不连续处的混合强度返回导致边界特征点定位不准确，影响标定精度。需要一种能显式处理边界展宽与畸变过渡带的标定方法。

Method: 1) 使用可打印平面靶标，视觉基准提供图像测量，圆形反射率边界提供LiDAR结构特征；2) 自动裁剪背景点、估计靶标平面，并迭代从强度与几何约束中精化3D特征点；3) 构建加权重投影一致性外参优化，融合LiDAR特征对齐与置信度加权残差；4) 利用估计的外参与提取的过渡带，通过边界重叠样本的俯仰-偏航-距离残差统计校准LiDAR边界响应。

Result: 在印刷板标定数据上实现了亚像素重投影精度、毫米级LiDAR特征一致性，并提升了里程计性能。

Conclusion: LV-Calib通过显式处理光束足印造成的边界畸变，实现了精确的LiDAR-相机外参标定与LiDAR边界响应校准，具有良好的可重复性和实际应用价值。

Abstract: We present LV-Calib, a calibration framework for LiDAR-camera extrinsic estimation and LiDAR boundary-response calibration using a printable planar target. The target serves as a shared observation carrier: visual fiducials provide indexed image measurements, while circular reflectivity boundaries provide LiDAR-observable structural feature points. Instead of directly fitting boundary points as ideal geometric contours, LV-Calib automatically crops background points, estimates the target plane, and iteratively refines accurate LiDAR-side 3-D feature points from intensity and geometric constraints. The refinement explicitly handles the broadened and distorted transition band induced by finite beam footprint and mixed-intensity returns around black-white reflectivity discontinuities. Given these refined LiDAR features, we formulate a weighted reprojection-consistent extrinsic optimization with LiDAR feature alignment, where image observations are kept in the reprojection domain and LiDAR feature residuals are weighted by refinement confidence. Finally, using the estimated extrinsic and the extracted transition band, LV-Calib calibrates the LiDAR boundary response by estimating pitch-yaw-range residual statistics of boundary-overlap samples. Experiments on printed-board calibration data demonstrate sub-pixel reprojection accuracy, millimeter-level LiDAR feature consistency, and improved odometry performance. Code and calibration data will be released for reproducible evaluation.

</details>


### [115] [Steering Autoregressive Vision-Language-Action Policies via Action Token Intervention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15021)
*Jason Chan,Jonathan C. Kao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Token Steering (TS)方法，通过在动作标记空间直接干预，实现自回归视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型轨迹的动态引导，无需额外训练或微调，在两项家庭操作任务上显著提升成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人基础模型缺乏轻量级、直观的用户干预手段，用户难以在保持模型灵巧性和任务先验知识的同时对轨迹生成进行有效引导。

Method: 将低维用户输入注入到VLA模型原生的动作标记表示中，在推理阶段直接干预动作标记空间，用户输入起到引导而非覆盖预训练策略的作用。

Result: 在抽屉关闭任务上成功率从10.0%提升至72.5%，在状态感知物体交换任务上从16.7%提升至93.8%。

Conclusion: TS方法提供了一种轻量级的机器人基础模型操控接口，有望改善消费环境人机交互，并扩展至身体控制能力受限用户的可用性。

Abstract: We present Token Steering (TS), a method for dynamically steering trajectories generated by an autoregressive vision-language-action (VLA) model through direct intervention in the action-token space. TS injects low-dimensional user inputs into the model's native action-token representation, allowing users to influence trajectory generation without modifying the underlying vision-language model (VLM) architecture. Because TS operates entirely at inference time, it requires no additional training or finetuning. User inputs guide rather than override the pretrained policy, allowing users to influence robot actions while preserving the dexterity, smoothness, and task priors learned by the VLA. We evaluate TS on two household manipulation tasks -- drawer closing after object placement and state-aware object swapping -- and improve success rates from 10.0% to 72.5% and from 16.7% to 93.8%, respectively. By enabling lightweight, intuitive steering over robot foundation models, our interface has the potential to improve human-robot interaction in consumer environments and broaden accessibility for individuals with limited physical control. Project website: https://jasontchan.github.io/token-steering/ .

</details>


### [116] [An Autonomous Subgram SMA-Based Swimmer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15028)
*Conor K. Trygstad,Francisco M. F. R. Gonçalves,Néstor O. Pérez-Arancibia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Swima，一种由形状记忆合金驱动的900毫克生物启发式微型游泳器，集成了机载电源和计算，能够自主游泳超过18分钟，速度达22.4毫米/秒，转弯速率14°/s，航向跟踪误差约6.5°，是首个低于1克且具备机载动力、驱动和计算的微型游泳机器人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现首个亚克级（subgram）具备完全机载动力、驱动和计算能力的自主微型游泳机器人，填补该尺度下自主微型机器人的空白。

Method: 采用两个10毫克的形状记忆合金（SMA）丝驱动的高功密（HWD）致动器作为推进源；集成定制PCB和11毫安时3.7伏507毫克单节锂离子电池实现机载供电与计算。

Result: Swima重量900毫克，自主游泳时长超18分钟，速度达22.4毫米/秒（0.56体长/秒），转弯速率14°/s，0°航向参考轨迹的均方根跟踪误差约6.5°。

Conclusion: Swima是迄今首个具备机载电源、驱动和计算的亚克级微型游泳机器人，展示了SMA驱动在微型自主水下机器人领域的可行性。

Abstract: We present the Swima, a bioinspired 900-mg swimmer propelled by two 10-mg high-work-density (HWD) actuators driven by shape-memory alloy (SMA) wires. We integrated onboard power and computation by using a custom-built printed circuit board (PCB) and an 11-mAh 3.7-V 507-mg single-cell lithium-ion (Li-Ion) battery, which in conjunction enable autonomous swimming in excess of 18 min. The Swima can swim at speeds of up to 22.4 mm/s (0.56 Bl/s), achieves turning rates of up to 14°/s, and can follow 0-degree heading reference trajectories with root mean square (RMS) values of tracking errors of about 6.5° across multiple tests. This robot is the first subgram microswimmer with onboard power, actuation, and computation developed to date.

</details>


### [117] [Exact, Efficient, and Safe Occlusion-Aware Planning Using AH-Polyhedrons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15046)
*Long Kiu Chung,David Isele,Toktam Mohammadnejad,Faizan M. Tariq,Sangjae Bae,Shreyas Kousik,Jovin D'sa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出APRO框架，利用AH-多面体可达性分析和博弈论主动感知，在自动驾驶代客泊车场景中实现精确、高效的遮挡感知规划，在保证100%安全率的同时保持实时性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遮挡感知规划方法存在缺乏形式化安全保障、假设智能体遵循道路结构或过于保守导致车辆卡住等问题，自动驾驶代客泊车场景中交通规则宽松、遮挡频繁杂乱，需要一种既安全又非保守的规划方法。

Method: 将先前工作中的集合安全条件重新表述为AH-多面体的并集，通过线性规划实现精确安全验证，避免集合计算中的额外保守性和对道路拓扑的假设；将安全条件集成到基于优化的规划器或二分搜索方案中实现实时应用。

Result: 在仿真和硬件实验中，包括真实停车场数据集回放测试，APRO在所有评估场景中均实现100%安全率，同时保持实时性能，比现有具有形式化安全保障的方法更安全、决策更优。

Conclusion: APRO通过AH-多面体可达性分析和博弈论主动感知，在无额外保守性假设下实现了精确的遮挡安全验证，为自动驾驶代客泊车提供了高效且理论上安全的解决方案。

Abstract: Safely handling occlusions is a fundamental challenge for autonomous mobile robots operating in dynamic environments. This issue is especially prominent in autonomous valet parking (AVP), where traffic rules are lax, occlusions are frequent and cluttered, and overly conservative behavior can leave vehicles stuck. However, existing methods either lack formal safety guarantees, assume agents follow road structures, or introduce conservatism, leaving occlusion-aware planning for AVP an open challenge. In this paper, we propose APRO (AH-Polyhedron Reachability for Occlusions), an exact and efficient occlusion-aware planning framework based on game-theoretic active perception and AH-polyhedron reachability analysis with AVP as our canonical use case. Our key insight is to reformulate set-based safety conditions in prior work as unions of AH-polyhedrons, enabling exact safety verification through linear programming (LP) without any additional conservatism in set computations or assumptions on road topology. We further show how the resulting safety conditions can be integrated into optimization-based planners or a bisection search scheme for real-time applications. We validate our method in simulation and hardware experiments, including data replay on a real-world parking lot dataset. Experimental results demonstrate that our method consistently achieved a 100% safety rate across all evaluated scenarios while maintaining real-time performance, resulting in safer and more optimal decisions than existing methods with formal safety guarantees.

</details>


### [118] [Design and Fabrication of a Spin Coater with In-Situ Optical Measurement for Soft Thin Films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15068)
*Daniel Gliksberg,Jiajie Qiu,Jun Suzuki,Kamal Youcef-Toumi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种低成本的、集成光学测厚系统的3D打印旋涂机，用于制造50-300微米的软质薄膜，厚度分辨率达3.6-3.7微米，重复性在10微米以内，并通过实验验证了其在硅橡胶薄膜制备中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旋涂是制备聚合物和弹性体薄膜的常用方法，但高柔性材料的厚度精确测量仍具挑战性——接触式测量会导致变形，传统光学测量成本高且复杂。在介电弹性体驱动器中，薄膜厚度直接影响机械和功能性能，因此需要低成本、非接触的厚度测量方案。

Method: 设计并制造了一台以3D打印件为主的台式旋涂机，集成了光学厚度测量系统：通过激光反射至四象限光电探测器来原位追踪位移，从而避免变形。对光学几何、传感器线性度进行了分析，并通过有限元分析验证结构可靠性。使用校准金属垫片进行实验验证。

Result: 系统厚度分辨率达3.6-3.7微米，最佳测量重复性为13微米（95%置信区间）。旋涂制备的硅橡胶薄膜厚度与目标值偏差在9微米以内。

Conclusion: 证明了可将低成本光学测量集成到旋涂系统中，实现对软质薄膜的厚度可控制造，无需专业工业仪器，具有良好的实用性和可及性。

Abstract: Spin coating is widely used for fabrication of thin polymer and elastomer films, yet reliable thickness verification of highly compliant materials remains challenging due to deformation from contact-based measurements and the cost and complexity of conventional optical metrology. Accurate thickness control is especially critical in soft elastomer applications such as dielectric elastomer actuators (DEAs), where mechanical and functional performance scales strongly with film thickness. This work presents a low-cost, primarily 3D-printed benchtop spin coater with an integrated, minimally deforming optical thickness measurement system for soft-film fabrication workflows. The system is designed to manufacture films between 50 and 300 microns thick with repeatability within 10 microns. Thickness is measured in-situ by tracking displacement of a reflected laser beam via quadrant photodetector, avoiding significant deformation. Optical geometry, sensor linearity constraints, and structural validation via finite element analysis are discussed. Experimental validation using calibrated metal shims demonstrated a thickness resolution of 3.6-3.7 microns and best-case measurement repeatability of 13 microns (95 percent confidence interval). The platform repeatably produced silicone films within 9 microns of target thickness, demonstrating that accessible optical metrology can be integrated into a low-cost spin coating system for practical, thickness-controlled fabrication of compliant thin films without specialized industrial instrumentation.

</details>


### [119] [DragMesh-2: Physically Plausible Dexterous Hand-Object Interaction with Articulated Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15133)
*Tianshan Zhang,Yijia Duan,Yanjun Li,Zeyu Zhang,Hao Tang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DragMesh-2是一个接触驱动的灵巧交互框架，将铰接物体从以物体为中心的运动生成扩展到以手驱动的灵巧手-物体交互，并提出PICA训练机制，无需触觉或力反馈即可将物理信号注入策略学习，提升接触负载变化下的鲁棒性和任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铰接物体操作与静态物体不同，目标部件无法直接驱动，其运动必须通过持续的手-手柄接触产生。从以物体为中心的铰接运动生成到手驱动的灵巧交互存在非平凡过渡，因为几何轨迹重放或开环执行无法建模驱动铰接部件所需的接触动力学。此外，仅在固定动力学下训练的策略会过拟合标称接触负载，缺乏触觉或力反馈时性能会下降。

Method: 提出DragMesh-2接触驱动框架，将铰接交互从以物体为中心的运动生成扩展到手驱动的灵巧手-物体交互；提出PICA（物理信息感知的接触感知训练机制），在无触觉或力反馈条件下将物理信号注入策略学习；在多种阻尼条件和铰接物体类别下进行系统评估。

Result: 在七个GAPartNet物体上，DragMesh-2在接触负载变化下比对比方法具有更强的鲁棒性，同时在多种阻尼条件下保持高任务成功率。

Conclusion: DragMesh-2有效解决了铰接物体灵巧操作中接触动力学建模和鲁棒性问题，并提供了纯几何灵巧交互资源以支持未来研究。

Abstract: Dexterous interaction with articulated objects is important for household, assistive, and humanoid manipulation, where multi-finger hands can provide compliant contact patterns beyond parallel-jaw grasping. However, articulated-object manipulation differs from static-object manipulation: the target part cannot be directly actuated, and its motion must emerge through sustained physical hand--handle contact. This makes the transition from object-centric articulated generation to hand-driven dexterous hand--object interaction non-trivial, since geometric trajectory replay or open-loop execution does not model the contact dynamics required to move the articulated part. Moreover, policies trained only for task completion under fixed dynamics can overfit nominal contact loads, especially without tactile or force feedback, and may degrade when the contact load changes. To address these challenges, we present DragMesh-2, a contact-driven framework for dexterous interaction with articulated objects that extends articulated interaction from object-centric generation to hand-driven dexterous hand--object interaction, where articulated motion must arise through physical contact. We further propose PICA, a physically informed contact-aware training mechanism that injects physical signals into policy learning without tactile or force feedback, improving robustness and task success under changing contact loads. Finally, we conduct systematic evaluation across multiple damping conditions and articulated-object categories to study robustness under contact-load variation, and provide a pure-geometry dexterous interaction resource to support future loco-manipulation and humanoid hand--object interaction research. Across seven GAPartNet objects, DragMesh-2 achieves stronger robustness under contact-load variation than the compared methods while maintaining high task success across damping conditions.

</details>


### [120] [MimicIK: Real-Time Generative Inverse Kinematics from Teleoperation with FK Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15148)
*Jiahao Yang,Shenhao Yan,Fan Feng,Chengsi Yao,Ge Wang,Zhixin Mai,Yiming Zhao,Yatong Han*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MimicIK是一种基于条件流匹配的实时生成式逆运动学框架，通过学习遥操作演示中的关节空间运动先验，实现高精度、平滑且鲁棒的实时机器人操控。在真实6-DOF机器人数据集上，MimicIK达到4.65mm位置误差、92.01%的10mm成功率，推理延迟仅6.74ms，支持20Hz实时控制，在奇异位形附近保持稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逆运动学(IK)仍是实时机器人操控的关键瓶颈：经典数值求解器虽几何精度高，但在闭环部署中常出现不连续分支切换和奇异点附近不稳定行为；而学习型IK方法在空间精度、运动平滑性和实时效率之间难以平衡，尤其是在噪声人机遥操作数据上训练时。

Method: 提出MimicIK框架：1) 基于条件流匹配(Conditional Flow Matching)从遥操作演示中学习平滑鲁棒的关节空间运动先验；2) 以当前关节构型和目标末端执行器位姿为条件，通过基于最小迭代策略(MIP)骨架的两步迭代精炼过程预测连续增量关节指令；3) 引入前向运动学一致性损失(FK consistency loss)，一种可微的正向运动学正则化项，在训练中惩罚任务空间相对于目标位姿的偏差。

Result: 在包含8,848条遥操作演示的真实6-DOF机器人数据集上，MimicIK实现：平均位置误差4.65mm，10mm成功率92.01%，轨迹尖峰率仅7.99%。相比UNet扩散基线，空间精度和运动平滑性均提升，推理延迟从21.66ms降至6.74ms。相比确定性MLP基线在分布外部署时灾难性发散，MimicIK在奇异位形附近保持稳定，支持20Hz实时控制。

Conclusion: MimicIK通过生成式条件流匹配有效解决了实时IK中精度、平滑性与效率的平衡问题，在奇异位形附近展现出强鲁棒性，验证了从遥操作演示学习关节空间先验用于实时机器人操控的可行性。

Abstract: Inverse kinematics (IK) remains a critical bottleneck for real-time robot manipulation. Classical numerical solvers achieve high geometric precision but often suffer from discontinuous branch switching and unstable behavior near kinematic singularities during closed-loop deployment. Meanwhile, learned IK approaches frequently struggle to balance spatial accuracy, motion smoothness, and real-time efficiency, particularly when trained on noisy human teleoperation data. We present \textbf{MimicIK}, a real-time generative inverse kinematics framework that learns smooth and robust joint-space motion priors from teleoperation demonstrations through conditional flow matching. Given the current joint configuration and a target end-effector pose, MimicIK predicts continuous delta-joint commands using an efficient two-step iterative refinement process based on a Minimal Iterative Policy (MIP) backbone. To enforce physical consistency, we further introduce an FK consistency loss, a differentiable forward-kinematics regularization that penalizes task-space deviations from the target pose during training. We evaluate MimicIK on a real-world 6-DOF robot dataset containing 8,848 teleoperation demonstrations. MimicIK achieves a mean position error of 4.65 mm, a 10 mm success rate of 92.01\%, and a trajectory spike rate of only 7.99\%. Compared with a UNet diffusion baseline, our method improves both spatial accuracy and motion smoothness while reducing inference latency from 21.66 ms to 6.74 ms. Furthermore, unlike deterministic MLP baselines that catastrophically diverge under out-of-distribution deployment, MimicIK remains stable near singular configurations and enables robust 20 Hz real-time control on deployment hardware.

</details>


### [121] [Task-Aware Environment Augmentation for Reliable Navigation via Shielded Conditional Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15154)
*Bharawee Phoompho,Gokul Puthumanaillam,Yan Miao,Ruben Hernandez,Tim Bretl,Sayan Mitra,Melkior Ornik*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SCoDA方法，通过条件扩散模型在环境中优化布置视觉标记（fiducial markers），以低成本的环境增强方式提升部分可观测条件下机器人轨迹执行的可靠性，解决了传统方法在观测匮乏区域状态估计不稳定的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在部分可观测环境下，可靠的轨迹规划不仅取决于几何路径的可行性，还依赖机器人执行过程中能否获得信息丰富的观测。现有方法通常固定环境而适配机器人（如信念空间规划、主动定位等），在观测匮乏区域容易产生脆弱行为且计算成本高。本文试图通过环境增强（在已建图环境中以少量视觉标记进行任务感知式增强）这一相对未充分探索的视角来解决问题。

Method: SCoDA（Shielded Conditional Diffusion for Environment Augmentation）——一种有屏蔽的条件扩散模型。它学习基于环境、规划轨迹、扰动上下文和期望执行剖面等条件的高性能标记布局条件分布。其屏蔽采样器（shielded sampler）推理沿轨迹应在何处进行位姿修正，将分布引导至任务相关且受限于有限预算的增强方案。

Result: 在模拟基准测试和硬件部署中，SCoDA在轨迹执行可靠性和完成时间上均优于强基线方法。

Conclusion: 少量精心布置的视觉标记即可提供足够的关键观测，防止不确定性在状态估计误差可能影响控制的区域累积，从而有效提升部分可观测下的轨迹执行可靠性。

Abstract: Reliable trajectory planning under partial observability depends not only on computing a feasible geometric path, but also on whether the robot receives informative observations while executing that trajectory. Existing approaches usually keep the environment fixed and adapt the robot through belief-space planning, active localization, or added sensing, often incurring costly uncertainty propagation and brittle behavior in observation-poor regions. We flip this perspective and address the largely open problem of \emph{task-aware environment augmentation}: given a mapped environment, a planned task trajectory, and a small budget of visual fiducial markers, where should the environment be augmented so that the planned trajectory can be executed reliably under uncertainty? Our key observation is that useful marker layouts are defined by the localization support they provide along the task trajectory: a small number of well-timed observations can be sufficient to prevent uncertainty from accumulating in regions where state-estimation error would otherwise compromise control. Building on this observation, we present \tbp{SCoDA}, $\textbf{S}$hielded $\textbf{Co}$nditional $\textbf{D}$iffusion for Environment $\textbf{A}$ugmentation. \tbp{SCoDA} learns a conditional distribution over high-performing fiducial layouts from data, using the environment, planned trajectory, disturbance context, and desired execution profile as conditioning. Its shielded sampler reasons over where along the planned execution pose corrections should occur, and steers this distribution toward task-relevant, finite-budget augmentations. Across simulated benchmarks and hardware deployments, we show that \tbp{SCoDA} improves trajectory execution reliability and completion time over strong baselines.
  Code, models and dataset available at: \hyperlink{scoda-diffusion.github.io}{https://scoda-diffusion.github.io/}

</details>


### [122] [Seam-to-Graph Reconstruction for Garment Configuration Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15171)
*Xuzhao Huang,Kai Tang,Fuyuki Tokuda,Norman C. Tien,Kazuhiro Kosuge*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出基于图神经网络和注意力机制的Seam-to-Graph网络，将部分可观测的服装接缝信息映射为拓扑编码的结构骨架图，实现实时服装状态估计，并设计形变感知的分层视觉伺服控制器，在双臂机器人上完成服装在丝网印刷平台上的精确对齐，达到人类级别的对齐精度且对不同服装具有鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 服装接缝编码了丰富的结构信息，但在机器人操作场景中通常只能部分观测到。现有方法未能充分利用接缝的结构信息来实现鲁棒的服装操作。

Method: 提出Seam-to-Graph网络，基于图神经网络和注意力机制将非结构化的接缝观测映射为拓扑编码的结构骨架图；基于骨架图状态估计设计形变感知的分层视觉伺服控制器，实现服装构型对齐。

Result: 在双臂机器人系统上，该方法达到人类级别的对齐精度，且对齐误差方差更小；对不同服装具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 利用接缝信息对于服装操作任务非常有效。

Abstract: Seams encode rich structural information about garments but are frequently partially observable in robotic manipulation scenarios. To robustly leverage seam information, we propose a Seam-to-Graph network based on graph neural networks and attention mechanisms. This network maps unstructured seam observations to a topology-encoded structural skeleton graph for real-time garment state estimation. Using this skeleton-graph-based state estimation, we design a deformation-aware, hierarchical visual servoing controller for garment configuration alignment. We implement this controller on a bimanual robot system to load a garment onto a screen printing platen and to align it to the desired configuration precisely. Real-robot experiments demonstrate that the robot using the proposed method not only achieves human-level alignment accuracy with reduced variance in alignment error but is also robust to different garments. These results demonstrate that the use of seam information is effective for garment manipulation.

</details>


### [123] [Rethinking Implicit Spatial Representation in Visuomotor Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15232)
*Xiangyu Chen,Yuxuan Hu,Chuhao Zhou,Jianfei Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文重新思考了空间softmax池化在机器人模仿学习中的作用，发现其能产生紧凑稳定的空间表征但受限于表征瓶颈，并提出PRISM——一种通过自顶向下交叉注意力融合保留多尺度隐式空间信息的视觉编码器，在低分辨率高精度任务上将成功率从5.0%提升至13.4%，仅增加15.4%参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管空间softmax表征已被广泛用于基于生成模型的机器人模仿学习策略中，但其有效性和内在机制尚未得到充分理解。本文旨在系统性地探究这种隐式空间表征是否能为机器人操作提供有效且稳定的视觉特征。

Method: 首先通过系统对比不同池化方法在视觉编码器中的表现，发现空间softmax池化优于特征值表征；然后通过显著性分析揭示其引导编码器聚焦任务相关区域的优势；最后针对表征瓶颈问题，提出PRISM视觉编码器，通过自顶向下的交叉注意力融合保留多尺度隐式空间信息。

Result: 在多个任务和策略主干上取得一致性改进。在低分辨率高精度的ToolHang任务上，平均成功率从5.0%提升至13.4%，参数量仅增加15.4%。

Conclusion: 多尺度隐式空间表征是机器人操作中有效且高效的设计原则，支持将其作为视觉编码器的核心设计方向。

Abstract: Generative model-based imitation learning has become a widely adopted paradigm for robotic manipulation, where policy performance depends critically on the conditioned visual representations. Although spatial softmax-based representations have been adopted in prior visuomotor policies, their effectiveness and underlying mechanisms remain insufficiently understood. This work rethinks the use of spatial softmax pooling: do such implicit spatial representations provide effective and stable visual features for robotic manipulation? Through systematic studies of different pooling methods in visual encoders, we find that this pooling operation produces compact and stable spatial representations, which outperform feature-value representations, despite using substantially fewer dimensions. Complementary saliency analysis further suggests that these spatial representations guide the encoder to focus more consistently on task-relevant regions. However, this advantage is limited by a representation bottleneck in current visual encoders: repeated downsampling operations weaken fine-grained spatial information before the action-generation module can use it, especially under low-resolution observations. Motivated by these findings, we propose PRISM, a visual encoder that preserves multiscale implicit spatial information through top-down cross-attention fusion. Experiments across multiple tasks and policy backbones show consistent improvements. In particular, on the low-resolution, high-precision ToolHang task, PRISM shows clear gains, improving the average success rate from 5.0% to 13.4% while increasing parameters by only 15.4%. These results support the use of multiscale implicit spatial representations as an effective and efficient design principle for robotic manipulation.

</details>


### [124] [Co-Creating Buildable and Open Social Robot Study Companions with University Students](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15239)
*Farnaz Baksh,Matevž B. Zorec,Feiazie Baksh,Karl Kruusamäe*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 通过DfA/DfD方法重新设计开源社交机器人底盘（快锁扣、卡扣、免工具卡扣），使系统可用性从差（SUS 59.4）提升至优秀（89.4），组装时间从21.4分钟降至13.7分钟，且感知工作量（而非完成时间）是决定学生是否采用开源硬件的关键因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源社交机器人虽具有可访问性、可修复性和学生赋能优势，但组装过程本身常构成障碍：现有平台要么预组装（剥夺动手学习机会），要么使用不熟悉的紧固件、混乱布线和难以触及的维护点，降低学生参与度。机械重设计能否在保持结构完整性的同时降低这一壁垒尚未得到验证。

Method: 运用双钻设计框架，与圭亚那和爱沙尼亚的大学生协同设计RSC v4.1底盘，采用扭锁式紧固件、卡扣连接和免工具维修闩锁。在两组研究（开发者与首次建造者）中通过SUS、NASA-TLX和计时评估效果。

Result: SUS得分从59.4提升至89.4（Poor→Excellent），NASA-TLX从4.29降至4.00，平均组装时间从21.4分钟降至13.7分钟（含新手学习效应）。方向提示与导航连续性成为文档化的下一个前沿。

Conclusion: 感知工作量（而非完成时间）是决定学生是否接受开源硬件的核心因素；DfA/DfD干预在缩短时间之前先改变了构建过程的"感受"。

Abstract: Open-source social robots offer accessibility, repairability, and student empowerment, yet the build itself often presents a barrier. Existing platforms either ship pre-assembled, foreclosing hands-on learning, or expose students to unfamiliar fasteners, opaque wiring, and inaccessible service points that erode engagement. Whether targeted mechanical redesign can lower this barrier whilst maintaining structural integrity remains untested. Here we show that Design for Assembly (DfA) and Design for Disassembly (DfD) interventions reshape how a build feels before they shorten how long it takes. Working with university students in Guyana and Estonia, we applied the Double Diamond framework to co-create the Robot Study Companion (RSC) v4.1: mapping pain points, then redesigning its chassis around twist-lock fasteners, snap-fit joints, and tool-free service latches. Across two studies with developers and first-time builders, system usability climbed from Poor to Excellent (SUS 59.4 to 89.4), perceived workload trended downward (NASA-TLX 4.29 to 4.00), and mean assembly time trended downward (21.4 to 13.7 minutes, with juniors' learning effect), whilst orientation cues and navigation continuity for first-time builders emerged as the next documentation frontier. Perceived workload, not completion time, appears to govern whether students take up open hardware.

</details>


### [125] [Driving, Fast or Slow? Neuro-Symbolic Guidance for Motion Prediction in Multi-Modal Ground Mobility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15251)
*Simon Kohaut,Felix Divo,Julius Hahnewald,Benedict Flade,Julian Eggert,Kristian Kersting,Devendra Singh Dhami*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TraCS是一个神经符号框架，通过可解释的概率一阶逻辑增强现有的黑盒运动预测模型，以在异构交通空间中实现合规性感知的轨迹预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有运动预测方法多为黑盒模型，缺乏对现实中交通法规和行为约束的显式编码，导致预测结果在安全关键场景下不可靠且不可解释。

Method: TraCS包含三个关键组件：(1) 智能体代码生成流水线，将自然语言交通规则转化为概率一阶逻辑；(2) 反应式数据流推理引擎，动态维护和更新合规性景观；(3) 神经置信度评分，学习上下文感知的合规信号衰减，防止过度修正。

Result: 在Argoverse 2基准测试上，TraCS一致地提升了现有最优运动预测骨干网络的性能。

Conclusion: 概率符号合规推理是纯神经运动预测器的通用且计算高效的补充方法，能够同时提升预测的准确性和可解释性。

Abstract: Accurate and interpretable motion prediction for heterogeneous traffic spaces, including pedestrians, bicycles, cars, and trucks, is essential for safe autonomous navigation. Nevertheless, state-of-the-art approaches remain predominantly black-box, lacking explicit encoding of the regulatory and behavioral constraints of real-world mobility. We propose Trajectory Compliance-Shaping (TraCS), a neuro-symbolic framework that augments existing black-box motion prediction backbones with interpretable and probabilistic first-order logic. To do so, TraCS employs an agentic code-generation pipeline to bridge the gap between natural-language descriptions of traffic regulations and probabilistic motion prediction. Furthermore, TraCS employs a reactive data-streaming inference engine that maintains and efficiently updates compliance landscapes as scenes evolve. To prevent TraCS from overconfidently steering the backbone's predictions in the wrong direction, we propose a neural confidence rating learned as a context-aware attenuation of the compliance signal. We demonstrate on the Argoverse 2 benchmark how TraCS consistently improves state-of-the-art prediction backbones, showing that probabilistic and symbolic compliance reasoning is a broadly applicable and computationally efficient complement to purely neural motion predictors.

</details>


### [126] [Impedance MPC for Physical Human-Robot Interaction: Predictive Disturbance Rejection with Joint-Limit Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08281)
*Yongyan Cao,Jinshan Tang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种双层阻抗MPC框架，通过第一层解析补偿动力学（重力、科氏力、惯性）将系统简化为配置无关的双积分器，第二层利用恒定结构求解30变量凸QP（100Hz），结合增广卡尔曼滤波器估计持续扰动，实现零稳态误差。在Franka FR3机器人上，稳态误差从44.8mm降至0.05mm以下（>800倍提升），同时保持亚毫米跟踪精度和对噪声/参数失配的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典阻抗控制在持续外力下存在非零稳态位置误差（力除以刚度），积分作用受限于窄稳定增益区间，无法同时满足轨迹精度和柔顺安全性。

Method: 双层结构：第一层解析补偿重力、科氏力和任务空间惯性，将残余系统简化为恒定状态转移矩阵的双积分器；第二层求解30变量凸QP（100Hz），利用恒定结构预计算自由响应矩阵，增广卡尔曼滤波器估计持续扰动状态以实现零稳态误差，通过零空间逆势垒和任务空间投影保证关节限位安全。

Result: 在7-DOF Franka FR3上，15N持续力下稳态误差0.05mm vs 44.8mm（>800倍降低），亚毫米3D圆轨迹跟踪，对测量噪声和30%惯性失配具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过解析补偿和恒定结构QP的巧妙结合，在不牺牲鲁棒性的前提下解决了阻抗控制中精度与柔顺的根本矛盾，实现了零稳态误差的物理人机交互。

Abstract: Physical human-robot interaction (pHRI) demands simultaneous trajectory accuracy and compliant safety under unplanned contact. Classical impedance control incurs a nonzero steady-state position error under sustained human force -- the applied force divided by the task stiffness -- which integral action reduces only within a narrow stable-gain budget. We present a two-layer Impedance MPC that resolves this tension. Layer~1 analytically cancels gravity, Coriolis, and task-space inertia, reducing the residual plant to a configuration-independent double integrator with a constant state-transition matrix. Layer~2 solves a 30-variable convex QP at 100\,Hz, exploiting this constant structure so the free-response matrix is precomputed once; an augmented Kalman filter estimates the persistent disturbance state, giving a formal zero-steady-state-error guarantee. A null-space inverse-barrier potential and a task-space workspace projection enforce joint-limit safety across the tested workspace. On a 7-DOF Franka FR3, Impedance MPC with Kalman augmentation attains sub-0.05\,mm steady-state error versus 44.8\,mm for classical impedance (a $>$800-fold reduction) under a sustained 15\,N force, sub-millimeter tracking on four 3-D circles, and graceful robustness to measurement noise and inertial mismatch up to 30\%.

</details>


### [127] [OSDAG: Online Scheduling for Efficient Multi-Robot Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15255)
*Thanh Nguyen Canh,Thang Tran Viet,Phuc Van Dinh,Xiem HoangVan,Nak Young Chong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OSDAG是一个将LLM与有向无环图（DAG）表示及在线调度相结合的框架，通过单次LLM推理将自然语言指令分解为依赖标注的任务图，再使用轻量级在线调度器实时分配就绪任务，从而避免重复LLM推理和离线预提交调度带来的延迟与空闲问题，在多个基准测试中实现了5-15倍推理加速和高达38%的完工时间缩减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的多机器人协调方法存在两个关键瓶颈：(1)执行过程中重复的LLM推理导致延迟随机器人数量增加而膨胀；(2)离线预提交的调度方式导致机器人必须等待顺序前置任务的完成，即使有独立工作可做也只能空闲等待。

Method: OSDAG框架将LLM任务推理与DAG表示及约束感知的在线调度相结合：LLM仅调用一次，将自然语言指令分解为带有依赖标注的任务图；轻量级在线调度器实时将就绪任务分配给空闲机器人。DAG表示编码了前置约束和资源约束，保证了正确性并暴露了所有可并行执行的任务。

Result: 在五个基准场景中，OSDAG相较于对话式方法实现了5-15倍的推理时间加速，相较于顺序基线方法减少了高达38%的完工时间，同时保持了具有竞争力的成功率。在双臂操作任务的仿真和真实实验中均验证了其有效性和实用性。

Conclusion: OSDAG通过单次LLM推理与轻量级在线调度相结合的方式，有效解决了异构多机器人系统中高层推理与低层调度之间的瓶颈问题，为复杂长时程任务的高效多机器人协调提供了一种实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Coordinating heterogeneous multi-robot systems (MRS) for complex, long-horizon tasks requires both flexible high-level reasoning and efficient low-level scheduling. Existing LLM-based approaches address the reasoning side but introduce two critical bottlenecks: (1) repeated LLM inference during execution, which inflates latency with agent count, and (2) offline, pre-committed scheduling, which forces robots to idle while waiting for sequentially ordered predecessors even when independent work is available. This paper presents OSDAG, a novel framework that integrates LLM-based task reasoning with Directed Acyclic Graph (DAG) representation and constraint-aware online scheduling. The LLM is invoked once to decompose a natural-language instruction into a dependency-annotated task graph, and a lightweight online scheduler then allocates ready tasks to idle agents in real time. The DAG representation encodes both precedence and resource constraints, ensuring correctness while exposing all available parallelism. Experiments across five benchmark scenarios demonstrate that OSDAG achieves 5-15x faster reasoning time compared to dialogue-based methods, reduces makespan by up to 38% over sequential baselines, and maintains competitive success rates. Both simulation and real-world experiments on dual-arm manipulation tasks validate the effectiveness and practicality of the proposed approach for efficient multi-robot coordination. The website and resources are available at http://thanhnguyencanh.github.io/LLM_DAG4MultiRobot

</details>


### [128] [Acting While Understanding: Asynchronous Semantic-Action Decoupling for Real-Time Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15285)
